[A] // Copyright 2013 The Foundry
[A] // Before using this shader the define MRI_CUBERENDER_NDOT_BATCH_SIZE must have been setup
[A] // Each entry in the batch corresponds to a mip-level for the NdotL texture (except that we're using array layers, rather than mips).
[A] in vec3 it_normal;
[A] out vec4 o_MriFragData[MRI_CUBERENDER_NDOT_BATCH_SIZE];
[A] uniform samplerCube s_texture;
[A] // This blur value needs to be in an array of structs as "uniform float s_blur[ MRI_CUBERENDER_NDOT_BATCH_SIZE];" does not upload uniforms correctly
[A] struct MriBlurData
[A] float value;
[A] uniform MriBlurData s_blur[MRI_CUBERENDER_NDOT_BATCH_SIZE];
[A] void accValues(in vec3 L,
[A] in vec3 N,
[A] in float roughness[MRI_CUBERENDER_NDOT_BATCH_SIZE],
[A] inout vec3 textureColour[MRI_CUBERENDER_NDOT_BATCH_SIZE],
[A] inout float sampleWeights[MRI_CUBERENDER_NDOT_BATCH_SIZE])
[A] float NdotL = dot(N, L);
[A] if( NdotL <= 0.0)
[A] return;
[A] vec3 texture0 = texture(s_texture, L.xyz).rgb;
[A] for (int indexBatch = 0; indexBatch < MRI_CUBERENDER_NDOT_BATCH_SIZE; ++indexBatch) {
[A] float weight = max(0.0, pow(NdotL, roughness[indexBatch]));
[A] textureColour[indexBatch].rgb += weight * texture0;
[A] sampleWeights[indexBatch] += weight;
[A] void main()
[A] vec3 normal0 = normalize(it_normal);
[A] vec3 textureColour0 = texture(s_texture, normal0.xyz).rgb;
[A] vec3 textureColour[MRI_CUBERENDER_NDOT_BATCH_SIZE];
[A] for (int indexBatch = 0 ; indexBatch < MRI_CUBERENDER_NDOT_BATCH_SIZE; ++indexBatch)
[A] textureColour[indexBatch] = textureColour0;
[A] ivec2 srcTextureSize = textureSize(s_texture, 0);
[A] ivec2 loopCount = min(ivec2(128, 128), srcTextureSize.xy);
[A] if (loopCount.x > 0 && loopCount.y > 0) {
[A] // Initialise the sampleWeights and roughness arrays.
[A] // roughness = 2^(13.0 * k) where
[A] //         k = pow(blur, 1.0 / levelPow) and
[A] //  levelPow = 2.0 (it's a variable, but always gets set to 2.0 in all code I could find)
[A] //      blur = mip level / (max mip level - 1)
[A] // so...
[A] // roughness = 2^(13.0 * sqrt(mip_level / (max_mip_level - 1)))
[A] // 0-1 gloss => roughness
[A] float roughness[MRI_CUBERENDER_NDOT_BATCH_SIZE];
[A] float sampleWeights[MRI_CUBERENDER_NDOT_BATCH_SIZE];
[A] roughness[indexBatch] = exp2(13.0 * s_blur[indexBatch].value);
[A] sampleWeights[indexBatch] = 1.0;
[A] // Sample the entire sphere to accumulate the colors for this pixel.
[A] // - Samples are evenly distributed over each of the 6 cube map faces,
[A] //   rather than evenly over the sphere
[A] // - There will be a set of samples for each face which are exactly on the
[A] //   cube seams.
[A] // - Samples are weighted by NdotL^roughness
[A] // - Samples where NdotL is less than or equal to zero can't contribute, so
[A] //   are wasted. The NdotL check in accValues rejects these samples before
[A] //   we read from the texture though.
[A] float blurLimit = 1.0;
[A] vec2 stepSize = vec2(2.0 * blurLimit) / loopCount;
[A] vec3 lerp = vec3(0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
[A] vec3 negateZ = vec3(1.0, 1.0, -1.0);
[A] for (lerp.x = -blurLimit; lerp.x <= blurLimit; lerp.x += stepSize.x) {
[A] for (lerp.y = -blurLimit; lerp.y <= blurLimit; lerp.y += stepSize.y) {
[A] vec3 dir = normalize(lerp);
[A] accValues(dir.xyz,           normal0, roughness, textureColour, sampleWeights);
[A] accValues(dir.xyz * negateZ, normal0, roughness, textureColour, sampleWeights);
[A] accValues(dir.yzx,           normal0, roughness, textureColour, sampleWeights);
[A] accValues(dir.yzx * negateZ, normal0, roughness, textureColour, sampleWeights);
[A] accValues(dir.zxy,           normal0, roughness, textureColour, sampleWeights);
[A] accValues(dir.zxy * negateZ, normal0, roughness, textureColour, sampleWeights);
[A] // Normalise each colour in the batch
[A] for(int indexBatch = 0; indexBatch < MRI_CUBERENDER_NDOT_BATCH_SIZE; ++indexBatch) {
[A] if (sampleWeights[indexBatch] > 0.0)
[A] textureColour[indexBatch].rgb = textureColour[indexBatch].rgb / vec3(sampleWeights[indexBatch]);
[A] // Copy calculated texture colours to the shader output
[A] for (int indexBatch = 0; indexBatch < MRI_CUBERENDER_NDOT_BATCH_SIZE; ++indexBatch)
[A] o_MriFragData[indexBatch] = vec4(textureColour[indexBatch].rgb, 1.0);
[A] // Copyright 2015 The Foundry
[A] in vec3 va_Vertex;
[A] in vec2 va_TexCoord;
[A] out vec2 vo_TexCoord;
[A] gl_Position = vec4(va_Vertex, 1.0);
[A] vo_TexCoord = va_TexCoord;

[A] vec4 Result = texture( s_texture, uv.xy);
[A] k#version 150
[A] //#include "noise2D.glslh"
[A] #include "ImportanceSample.glslh"
[A] uniform samplerCube u_SamplerEnvMap;
[A] uniform float u_Roughness;
[A] uniform float u_RoughnessScale;
[A] uniform uint u_NumSamples;
[A] in vec3 vo_Vertex;
[A] // Code which uses this shader can #define GNZ_BRDF to one of the following
[A] // values:
[A] // - 0 = "Traditional" shading model, a.k.a. Blinn-Phong
[A] // - 1 = "Energy Conserving" shading model, a.k.a. Ashikmin-Shirley
[A] // - 2 = "Physically-based" shading model. Uses Generalised Trowbridge-Reitz NDF, Schlick for Fresnel
[A] // - 3 = Lambertian diffuse BRDF
[A] // - 4 = Oren-Nayar diffuse BRDF
[A] #ifndef GNZ_BRDF
[A] #define GNZ_BRDF 0
[A] #define UNDEF_GNZ_BRDF 1
[A] #define M_PI     3.14159265358979
[A] #define M_INV_PI 0.31830988618379
[A] vec3 environment(vec3 dir, float lod)
[A] return textureLod(u_SamplerEnvMap, dir, lod).rgb;
[A] float calculateLOD(float roughness, float NdotH, float VdotH, float solidAngleTexel)
[A] float pdf = pdfBRDF(roughness, NdotH, VdotH);
[A] float solidAngleSample = 1.0 / (float(u_NumSamples) * max(pdf, 1e-6));
[A] float lod = (roughness == 0.0) ? 0.0 : 0.5 * log2(solidAngleSample / solidAngleTexel);
[A] return lod;
[A] float solidAngleForTexel(vec2 fragCoord, ivec2 resolution)
[A] float dTheta = 2.0 * M_PI / float(resolution.x);
[A] float phiSouth = (floor(fragCoord.y      ) / float(resolution.y + 1) - 0.5) * M_PI;
[A] float phiNorth = (floor(fragCoord.y + 1.0) / float(resolution.y + 1) - 0.5) * M_PI;
[A] return max(dTheta * (sin(phiNorth) - sin(phiSouth)), 1e-6);
[A] vec3 prefilterEnvMap(float roughness, vec3 vertexCoord)
[A] vec3 prefilteredColor = vec3(0.0);
[A] float totalWeight = 0.0;
[A] // These are needed for calculating the correct MIP-level to sample from.
[A] ivec2 cubeSideTextureSize = textureSize(u_SamplerEnvMap, 0).xy;
[A] float solidAngleTexel = solidAngleForTexel(gl_FragCoord.xy, cubeSideTextureSize);
[A] //get the direction of our texel in screen space, i.o.w, we have a texel, that gives us a u,v into our texture cube side
[A] //its direction, is just itself?
[A] vec3 N = normalize(vertexCoord);
[A] mat3 ttw = tangentToWorld(N);
[A] for (uint i = 0u; i < u_NumSamples; ++i) {
[A] vec2 Xi = hammersley(i, u_NumSamples);
[A] // Seed the y coord.
[A] // Xi.y = fract(Xi.y + seed);
[A] #if GNZ_BRDF >= SHADING_DIFFUSE_START_INDEX
[A] // for the Lambertian and Oren-Nayar BRDF, sample the
[A] // light direction directly instead of the half vector.
[A] vec3 L = ttw * importanceSampleBRDF(Xi, roughness);
[A] vec3 H = normalize(L + N);
[A] #else
[A] vec3 H = ttw * importanceSampleBRDF(Xi, roughness);
[A] vec3 L = reflect(-N, H);
[A] if (NdotL > 0.0) {
[A] // Calculate the correct LOD level to sample the source texture from.
[A] // The LOD level is determined by the probability of the sample, p, and
[A] // the distortion factor for the pixel at the sample location.
[A] float NdotH = dot(N, H);
[A] float lod = calculateLOD(roughness, NdotH, NdotH, solidAngleTexel);
[A] // #if GNZ_BRDF > 2 // if we're integrating a diffuse BRDF
[A] // lod += 2.0;
[A] // #endif
[A] prefilteredColor += environment(L, lod) * NdotL;
[A] totalWeight += NdotL;
[A] return prefilteredColor / totalWeight;
[A] vec3 color = prefilterEnvMap(u_Roughness, vo_Vertex);
[A] out_FragColor = vec4(color, 1.0);
[A] #ifdef UNDEF_GNZ_BRDF
[A] #undef GNZ_BRDF
[A] #undef UNDEF_GNZ_BRDF
[A] B#version 150
[A] uniform mat4x4 u_MriModelViewProjectionMatrix;
[A] uniform mat3x3 u_MriNormalMatrix;
[A] in vec4 mri_Vertex;
[A] in vec3 mri_Normal;
[A] out vec3 it_normal;
[A] gl_Position = u_MriModelViewProjectionMatrix * mri_Vertex;
[A] it_normal   = u_MriNormalMatrix * mri_Normal;
[A] )#version 150
[A] uniform sampler2D u_SamplerEnvMap;
[A] #ifndef SOLID_SAMPLING
[A] #define SOLID_SAMPLING 0
[A] #define UNDEF_SOLID_SAMPLING 1
[A] vec2 uvToLatLong(vec2 uv)
[A] return (uv - 0.5) * vec2(2.0, 1.0) * M_PI;
[A] vec3 latLongToDirection(vec2 latlong)
[A] float phi = latlong.x;
[A] float theta = latlong.y;
[A] float cosTheta = cos(theta);
[A] return vec3(cos(phi) * cosTheta, sin(theta), sin(phi) * cosTheta);
[A] vec2 directionToLatLong(vec3 dir)
[A] float phi = atan(dir.z, dir.x);
[A] float theta = asin(dir.y);
[A] return vec2(phi, theta);
[A] vec2 latLongToUV(vec2 latlong)
[A] return latlong * vec2(0.5, 1.0) * M_INV_PI + 0.5;
[A] vec2 uv = latLongToUV(directionToLatLong(dir));
[A] return textureLod(u_SamplerEnvMap, uv, lod).rgb;
[A] float solidAngleForLatLongTexel(vec2 fragCoord, ivec2 resolution)
[A] vec2 sampleToLatLong(ivec2 texSize, uint sampleNum)
[A] // get (x,y) coordinates given current sample number and texture size.
[A] float quotient = floor(float(sampleNum)/float(texSize.x));
[A] float remainder = mod(float(sampleNum), float(texSize.x));
[A] // +0.5 to make coordinate relative to texel centre instead of edge
[A] return vec2(0.5) + vec2(remainder, quotient);
[A] vec3 prefilterEnvMap(float roughness, vec3 N)
[A] vec3 V = N;
[A] ivec2 texSize = textureSize(u_SamplerEnvMap, 0).xy;
[A] float solidAngleTexel = solidAngleForLatLongTexel(gl_FragCoord.xy, texSize);
[A] //float seed = snoise(gl_FragCoord.xy);
[A] #if SOLID_SAMPLING == 0
[A] vec2 Xi = sampleToLatLong(texSize, i);
[A] vec3 L = latLongToDirection(Xi);
[A] vec3 H = normalize(L + V);
[A] vec3 L = reflect(-V, H);
[A] float VdotH = dot(V, H);
[A] float lod = calculateLOD(roughness, NdotH, VdotH, solidAngleTexel);
[A] vec3 dir = normalize(latLongToDirection(uvToLatLong(vo_TexCoord)));
[A] vec3 color = prefilterEnvMap(u_Roughness, dir);
[A] #ifdef UNDEF_SOLID_SAMPLING
[A] #undef SOLID_SAMPLING
[A] #undef UNDEF_SOLID_SAMPLING
[A] 3#version 150
[A] #ifndef M_PI
[A] #define M_PI      3.14159265358979
[A] #define UNDEF_PI  1
[A] #ifndef M_INV_PI
[A] #define M_INV_PI     0.31830988618379
[A] #define UNDEF_INV_PI 1
[A] #if !defined(DRAW_BUFFER_USE_BASE_INSTANCE)
[A] uniform uint u_DrawID;
[A] return u_DrawID;
[A] vec3 gammaCorrect(vec3 color, float gamma)
[A] return pow(color, vec3(1.0 / gamma));
[A] vec4 gammaCorrect(vec4 color, float gamma)
[A] return vec4(gammaCorrect(color.rgb, gamma), color.a);
[A] mat3 mat3Rotation(mat4 m)
[A] return transpose(inverse(mat3(m)));
[A] /// Convert a world-space direction vector into UV coordinates suitable for
[A] /// accessing a lat-long texture map.
[A] ///
[A] /// The dir parameter is a world space vector in a right-handed coordinate
[A] /// system where up is along the +ve y axis. If your input vector uses Z-up,
[A] /// you'll need to convert it to Y-up first.
[A] /// Returns a vec2 where the x value represents a rotation around the y axis
[A] /// and the y direction represents a rotation around the x axis.
[A] /// The x value encodes a rotation between -180 degrees at x = 0.0 and
[A] /// +180 degrees at x = 1.0. The y value is a rotation between -90 degrees at
[A] /// y = 0.0 and +90 degrees y = 1.0.
[A] /// The horizontal rotation starts from the +ve x axis and moves clockwise
[A] /// around the y axis; the vertical rotation starts from the x-z plane and
[A] /// moves clockwise around the x axis. These both assume a right-handed
[A] /// coordinate system. The cardinal directions are:
[A] /// - forward: x = 0.25, y = 0.5
[A] /// - back:    x = 0.75, y = 0.5
[A] /// - left:    x = 0.00, y = 0.5
[A] /// - right:   x = 0.50, y = 0.5
[A] /// - up:      x = 0.25, y = 1.0
[A] /// - down:    x = 0.25, y = 0.0
[A] /// Note that x values wrap (x = 1.0 means a +180 degree rotation, which gives
[A] /// the same result as a -180 degree rotation, which corresponds to x = 0.0). Y
[A] /// values don't wrap.
[A] return vec2(phi*0.5, theta) * M_INV_PI + 0.5;
[A] /// Modo has an incorrect conversion from direction to lat-long coords which
[A] /// causes lat-long textures to be flipped horizontally. This code matches it.
[A] /// The input and the result are in the same formats as the non-Modo version of
[A] /// this function, described above.
[A] vec2 directionToLatLong_Modo(vec3 dir)
[A] float phi = atan(-dir.z, dir.x) + 0.5 * M_PI;
[A] #ifdef UNDEF_PI
[A] #undef M_PI
[A] #undef UNDEF_PI
[A] #ifdef UNDEF_INV_PI
[A] #undef M_INV_PI
[A] #undef UNDEF_INV_PI
[A] /#version 150
[A] // Copyright 2024 Foundry
[A] layout (pixel_center_integer) in vec4 gl_FragCoord;
[A] uniform vec2 u_VpSize;
[A] ivec2 src_size = textureSize(u_Src, 0);
[A] vec2 scale = vec2(src_size) / u_VpSize; // takes us from dst->src space
[A] vec2 dst_coord = gl_FragCoord.xy;
[A] vec2 src_coord = dst_coord * scale;
[A] // bl = bottom left
[A] // tr = top right
[A] // (gl_FragCoord origin is bottom-left!)
[A] vec2 bl = src_coord;
[A] vec2 tr = src_coord + vec2(1, 1) * scale;
[A] // 'whole' == whole pixel, any pixel x where `whole_bl <= x < whole_tr` is a
[A] // whole pixel + the rest are weighted
[A] ivec2 whole_bl = ivec2( ceil(bl));
[A] ivec2 whole_tr = ivec2(floor(tr));
[A] vec4 sum = vec4(0, 0, 0, 0);
[A] // area of texels sampled from src, in texels (1 == 1 whole texel sampled)
[A] // whole-pixel parts first
[A] ivec2 pos;
[A] for (pos.y = whole_bl.y; pos.y < whole_tr.y; pos.y += 1) {
[A] for (pos.x = whole_bl.x; pos.x < whole_tr.x; pos.x += 1) {
[A] sum += texelFetch(u_Src, ivec2(pos.x, pos.y), 0);
[A] // Size is in pixels, should always be between 0 and 1, used as a weight for
[A] // that pixel.
[A] float left_fringe_size   = float(whole_bl.x) - bl.x;
[A] float right_fringe_size  = tr.x              - float(whole_tr.x);
[A] float bottom_fringe_size = float(whole_bl.y) - bl.y;
[A] float top_fringe_size    = tr.y              - float(whole_tr.y);
[A] float total_weight = (whole_tr.x - whole_bl.x) * (whole_tr.y - whole_bl.y);
[A] // fringes, aka half-pixels at the edges but *not* the corners
[A] // left/right first
[A] for (int yy = whole_bl.y; yy < whole_tr.y; yy += 1) {
[A] // fringe size should be 0 if we're exactly on a pixel, so we max(0, ...)
[A] // here to avoid sampling off the edge of  the texture but we're assuming
[A] // the fringe size is 0 so the result shouldn't matter.
[A] sum += texelFetch(u_Src, ivec2(max(0,              whole_bl.x - 1), yy), 0) * left_fringe_size;
[A] sum += texelFetch(u_Src, ivec2(min(src_size.x - 1, whole_tr.x    ), yy), 0) * right_fringe_size;
[A] total_weight += left_fringe_size + right_fringe_size;
[A] // top/bottom
[A] for (int xx = whole_bl.x; xx < whole_tr.x; xx += 1) {
[A] sum += texelFetch(u_Src, ivec2(xx, max(0,              whole_bl.y - 1)), 0) * bottom_fringe_size;
[A] sum += texelFetch(u_Src, ivec2(xx, min(src_size.y - 1, whole_tr.y    )), 0) * top_fringe_size;
[A] total_weight += top_fringe_size + bottom_fringe_size;
[A] // corners
[A] float tl_size = top_fringe_size    * left_fringe_size;
[A] float tr_size = top_fringe_size    * right_fringe_size;
[A] float bl_size = bottom_fringe_size * left_fringe_size;
[A] float br_size = bottom_fringe_size * right_fringe_size;
[A] sum += texelFetch(u_Src, ivec2(max(0,              whole_bl.x - 1), max(0,              whole_bl.y - 1)), 0) * bl_size;
[A] sum += texelFetch(u_Src, ivec2(min(src_size.x - 1, whole_tr.x    ), max(0,              whole_bl.y - 1)), 0) * br_size;
[A] sum += texelFetch(u_Src, ivec2(max(0,              whole_bl.x - 1), min(src_size.y - 1, whole_tr.y    )), 0) * tl_size;
[A] sum += texelFetch(u_Src, ivec2(min(src_size.x - 1, whole_tr.x    ), min(src_size.y - 1, whole_tr.y    )), 0) * tr_size;
[A] total_weight += tl_size + tr_size + bl_size + br_size;
[A] out_FragColor = sum / total_weight;

[A] vU!

[A] Y,4w

[A] RS=
[A] QH#
[A] +HEIR
[A] _>,m
[A] uniform uint u_NumLODLevels;
[A] out vec3 out_FragColor;
[A] vec3 integrateBRDF(float roughness, float NdotV)
[A] // Assuming that N points along the +ve z axis, find V
[A] float sinTheta = sqrt(1.0 - NdotV * NdotV);
[A] vec3 N = vec3(0.0, 0.0, 1.0);
[A] vec3 V = vec3(sinTheta, 0.0, NdotV);
[A] vec3 lutValue = vec3(0.0, 0.0, max(0.0, float(u_NumLODLevels) * roughness));
[A] vec3 L = importanceSampleBRDF(Xi, roughness);
[A] vec3 H = importanceSampleBRDF(Xi, roughness);
[A] float NdotL = clamp(L.z, 0.0, 1.0);
[A] float NdotH = clamp(H.z, 0.0, 1.0);
[A] lutValue.xy += evaluateBRDF(L, V, H, roughness, NdotV, NdotL, NdotH);
[A] lutValue.xy /= float(u_NumSamples);
[A] return lutValue;
[A] float NdotV = vo_TexCoord.x;
[A] float roughness = vo_TexCoord.y;
[A] out_FragColor = integrateBRDF(roughness, NdotV);
[A] out vec3 vo_Vertex;
[A] uniform mat3 u_Transform;
[A] vo_Vertex = va_Vertex*u_Transform;
[A] G#version 150
[A] // Description : Array and textureless GLSL 2D simplex noise function.
[A] //      Author : Ian McEwan, Ashima Arts.

[A] //               https://github.com/ashima/webgl-noise
[A] vec3 mod289(vec3 x) {
[A] return x - floor(x * (1.0 / 289.0)) * 289.0;
[A] vec2 mod289(vec2 x) {
[A] vec3 permute(vec3 x) {
[A] return mod289(((x*34.0)+1.0)*x);
[A] float snoise(vec2 v)
[A] const vec4 C = vec4(0.211324865405187,  // (3.0-sqrt(3.0))/6.0
[A] 0.366025403784439,  // 0.5*(sqrt(3.0)-1.0)
[A] -0.577350269189626,  // -1.0 + 2.0 * C.x
[A] 0.024390243902439); // 1.0 / 41.0
[A] // First corner
[A] vec2 i  = floor(v + dot(v, C.yy) );
[A] vec2 x0 = v -   i + dot(i, C.xx);
[A] // Other corners
[A] vec2 i1;
[A] //i1.x = step( x0.y, x0.x ); // x0.x > x0.y ? 1.0 : 0.0
[A] //i1.y = 1.0 - i1.x;
[A] i1 = (x0.x > x0.y) ? vec2(1.0, 0.0) : vec2(0.0, 1.0);
[A] // x0 = x0 - 0.0 + 0.0 * C.xx ;
[A] // x1 = x0 - i1 + 1.0 * C.xx ;
[A] // x2 = x0 - 1.0 + 2.0 * C.xx ;
[A] vec4 x12 = x0.xyxy + C.xxzz;
[A] x12.xy -= i1;
[A] // Permutations
[A] i = mod289(i); // Avoid truncation effects in permutation
[A] vec3 p = permute( permute( i.y + vec3(0.0, i1.y, 1.0 ))
[A] + i.x + vec3(0.0, i1.x, 1.0 ));
[A] vec3 m = max(0.5 - vec3(dot(x0,x0), dot(x12.xy,x12.xy), dot(x12.zw,x12.zw)), 0.0);
[A] m = m*m ;
[A] // Gradients: 41 points uniformly over a line, mapped onto a diamond.
[A] // The ring size 17*17 = 289 is close to a multiple of 41 (41*7 = 287)
[A] vec3 x = 2.0 * fract(p * C.www) - 1.0;
[A] vec3 h = abs(x) - 0.5;
[A] vec3 ox = floor(x + 0.5);
[A] vec3 a0 = x - ox;
[A] // Normalise gradients implicitly by scaling m
[A] // Approximation of: m *= inversesqrt( a0*a0 + h*h );
[A] m *= 1.79284291400159 - 0.85373472095314 * ( a0*a0 + h*h );
[A] // Compute final noise value at P
[A] vec3 g;
[A] g.x  = a0.x  * x0.x  + h.x  * x0.y;
[A] g.yz = a0.yz * x12.xz + h.yz * x12.yw;
[A] return 130.0 * dot(m, g);
[A] uniform samplerCube u_Texture;
[A] out_FragColor = texture(u_Texture, vo_Vertex);
[A] w#version 150
[A] in vec2 va_Vertex;
[A] gl_Position = vec4(va_Vertex, 0.0, 1.0);

[A] #define DRAW_ID(node) uint(node.a)
[A] #define INTERPOLATE(Val0,Val1,Val2,BarycentricCoords) (BarycentricCoords.x*Val0+BarycentricCoords.y*Val1+BarycentricCoords.z*Val2)
[A] vec4 positionFromDepth(vec2 uv, float depth) {
[A] float x = uv.x * 2.0 - 1.0;
[A] float y = uv.y * 2.0 - 1.0;
[A] float z = depth * 2.0 - 1.0;
[A] vec4 projectedPos = vec4(x, y, z, 1.0);
[A] vec4 pos = inverse(getModelMatrix()) * u_InvCameraMatrix * u_InvProjMatrix * projectedPos;
[A] return pos / pos.w;
[A] vec3 barycentricCoords(vec3 a, vec3 b, vec3 c, vec3 p)
[A] vec3 v0 = b - a;
[A] vec3 v1 = c - a;
[A] vec3 v2 = p - a;
[A] float d00 = dot(v0, v0);
[A] float d01 = dot(v0, v1);
[A] float d11 = dot(v1, v1);
[A] float d20 = dot(v2, v0);
[A] float d21 = dot(v2, v1);
[A] float invDenom = 1.0 / (d00 * d11 - d01 * d01);
[A] vec3 bc;
[A] bc.y = (d11 * d20 - d01 * d21) * invDenom;
[A] bc.z = (d00 * d21 - d01 * d20) * invDenom;
[A] bc.x = 1.0f - bc.y - bc.z;
[A] return bc;
[A] void initializeVisibilityBufferQuery(vec2 ndcCoord, inout uint pointer)
[A] pointer = texelFetch(u_SamplerHeadPointer,ivec2(gl_FragCoord.xy),0).r;
[A] void getFragmentInfo(vec2 ndcCoord, inout uint pointer, out vec4 position, out float depth, out vec3 normal, out vec2 texCoord)
[A] uvec4  node = texelFetch(u_SamplerNodeBuffer,ivec2(
[A] int(headPointer-1u)%u_SamplerNodeBufferWidth,
[A] int(headPointer-1u)/u_SamplerNodeBufferWidth
[A] ),0);
[A] if(headPointer==0u)
[A] discard;
[A] uint pointer = headPointer;
[A] while(pointer!=0u)
[A] uvec4 node = texelFetch(u_SamplerNodeBuffer,ivec2(
[A] int(pointer-1u)%u_SamplerNodeBufferWidth,
[A] int(pointer-1u)/u_SamplerNodeBufferWidth
[A] pointer = node.r;
[A] uint drawID = DRAW_ID(node);
[A] g_DrawID = drawID;
[A] depth = DEPTH(node);
[A] position = positionFromDepth(ndcCoord, depth);
[A] int primitiveID = PRIMITIVE_ID(node);
[A] int VertexIndex[3];
[A] if(u_indexedDraw)
[A] int index = int(u_DrawElementsIndirectCommands[drawID].firstIndex) + primitiveID*3;
[A] int baseVertex = int(u_DrawElementsIndirectCommands[drawID].baseVertex);
[A] VertexIndex[0] = baseVertex + texelFetch(u_IndexBuffer,index+0).r;
[A] VertexIndex[1] = baseVertex + texelFetch(u_IndexBuffer,index+1).r;
[A] VertexIndex[2] = baseVertex + texelFetch(u_IndexBuffer,index+2).r;
[A] else
[A] VertexIndex[0] = primitiveID*3+0;
[A] VertexIndex[1] = primitiveID*3+1;
[A] VertexIndex[2] = primitiveID*3+2;
[A] vec3 barycentricCoords = barycentricCoords(
[A] texelFetch(u_VertexBuffer,VertexIndex[0]).xyz,
[A] texelFetch(u_VertexBuffer,VertexIndex[1]).xyz,
[A] texelFetch(u_VertexBuffer,VertexIndex[2]).xyz,
[A] position.xyz
[A] normal = INTERPOLATE(
[A] texelFetch(u_NormalBuffer,VertexIndex[0]).xyz,
[A] texelFetch(u_NormalBuffer,VertexIndex[1]).xyz,
[A] texelFetch(u_NormalBuffer,VertexIndex[2]).xyz,
[A] barycentricCoords
[A] texCoord = INTERPOLATE(
[A] texelFetch(u_texCoordBuffer,VertexIndex[0]).xy,
[A] texelFetch(u_texCoordBuffer,VertexIndex[1]).xy,
[A] texelFetch(u_texCoordBuffer,VertexIndex[2]).xy,
[A] vec3 outLocation = u_Transform * va_Vertex;
[A] vo_Vertex = outLocation;
[A] vec3 n = normalize( it_normal);
[A] vec2 SphericalCoords = (0.5*n.xy) + vec2( 0.5);

[A] #ifndef GNZ_GLSL_SHADER_BIT_ENCODING_GLSLH
[A] #define GNZ_GLSL_SHADER_BIT_ENCODING_GLSLH
[A] #if defined(GL_ARB_shader_bit_encoding)
[A] #extension GL_ARB_shader_bit_encoding : enable
[A] uint floatBitsToUint(float f)
[A] float Abs = abs(f);
[A] float Sign = step(0.0,-f);
[A] float Exponent = floor(log2(Abs));
[A] float Mantissa = (exp2(- Exponent) * Abs);
[A] Exponent = floor(log2(Abs) + 127.0) + floor(log2(Mantissa));
[A] //"  return uint(exp2(31u))*uint(Sign) + uint(exp2(23u))*uint(Exponent) + uint(Mantissa) * uint(exp2(22u));
[A] return exp2(31u)*Sign + exp2(23u)*Exponent + Mantissa * exp2(22u);
[A] float uintBitsToFloat(uint u)
[A] float Sign = floor(u*exp2(-31));
[A] float Mantissa = mod(u,exp2(23));
[A] float Exponent = (mod(u,exp2(31))-Mantissa) * exp2(-23);
[A] Mantissa = Mantissa / exp2(22);
[A] return (Sign*2.0-1.0)  *  exp2(Exponent-127.0)  *  Mantissa;
[A] #endif //GNZ_GLSL_SHADER_BIT_ENCODING_GLSLH
